| D-77 | doctrine | 2026-07-30 | **Goal-directed work is where a case turns into a brief, and nothing guards against it.** Now invariant 7 of the framework: a goal may direct what is SOUGHT but must never filter what is recorded, retained or shown about what was found, and a finding that cuts against a goal must be surfaced at least as prominently as one that supports it. This is the invariant most likely to be violated BY ACCIDENT, because helpfulness looks exactly like it from the inside: an assistant pursuing an objective, ranking findings by relevance to that objective, is one short step from an assistant that quietly does not mention the inconvenient one. `BIO_Declared_Bias_v0_1.md` is the doctrine this connects to and neither is implemented. The concrete mechanism needed is in Step 8b: every finding takes the same path to a member regardless of which way it cuts, and a deferral is recorded with its reason. | DOCTRINE · CLOSED 2026-09-21 BY THE THIRD DOOR, SCHEDULER #10 draining BOB #25's answer to Q1 of DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB (`37367611`): its doctrine is invariant 7 and its guard is a STATED DEFERRAL, read there: `BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md`'s front matter (Incomplete sections), §12, whose design act must carry invariant 7 as the ACCEPTANCE of the first row that lets any goal or aspiration order anything. Nothing orders findings by a goal today because no goal exists, and a member's deferral already records its reason (`proposal_dispositions.reason` and `finding_dispositions.reason` are NOT NULL); its trigger: SCHEDULER schedules the design act when `node tools/status.mjs 12` reads the publication ceremony and the accept surface BUILT, both ABSENT today, or earlier on Bob's word. **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** DOCTRINE (Bob) · open — **SENT 2026-09-21 by SCHEDULER #10 to BOB, Q1 of a group of three (DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB)**, verified at the code on `7a8b81d6`. |
| D-79 | design | 2026-07-30 | **Unattended surfacing needs aggregation and ageing, neither of which exists.** An assistant that can open a focus without a member can produce hundreds where a person produces one, and framework §12 states the two disciplines that requires. AGGREGATE, DO NOT MULTIPLY: one junction check firing across 58 contracts is one focus with 58 instances, not 58 focuses, because "why do these 58 awards have no solicitation" is one question; getting this wrong does not corrupt the record, it drowns it, which for an advisory object is the same failure. AGE RATHER THAN VANISH: a machine-surfaced focus nobody has acted on after some interval moves to `deferred` with the reason recorded, never silently disappearing, because a finding that disappears is indistinguishable from one that was never made and that rule does not relax because the finder was a machine. Neither the aggregation key nor the ageing job exists. | M4 · CLOSED 2026-09-21 BY THE THIRD DOOR, SCHEDULER #10 draining BOB #25's answer to Q1 of DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB (`37367611`): BUILT for derived findings and a STATED DEFERRAL for an assistant's questions, read at `BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md`'s front matter (Incomplete sections), §12: `op=proposals` is one proposal per progression and stage carrying its instances (REC-6), and `proposal_dispositions` ages one only by a member's recorded decision (REC-7); an assistant's questions are fenced by `INVESTIGATIVE-SESSION.md` §11 item 5, and their aggregation and ageing are deferred to the first role whose output is a new inquiry. **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** M4 · open — **SENT 2026-09-21 by SCHEDULER #10 to BOB, Q1 of a group of three (DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB)**, verified at the code on `7a8b81d6`. |
| D-81 | design | 2026-07-30 | **An aspiration accumulates learning and has nowhere to put it.** Follows from Bob's ruling that we learn from pursuing aspirations whether or not they are achieved. An object that never closes has no natural moment at which anyone writes down what it taught, so framework §12.2 adds a pursuit record: the objectives opened under it, the findings adopted and deferred, the records requests and their answers, the measurements, and the dead ends. Three properties are the whole value and each is easy to lose. A DEAD END IS KEPT, since "we assumed the fund code would appear in the procurement portal and it does not" is the kind of thing every member relearns individually and nobody writes down, and it is exactly what makes the next member's work cheaper. It is NOT A PROGRESS BAR, since an aspiration has no completion; objectives beneath it carry satisfaction conditions and derived progress, the aspiration itself does not. And it SURVIVES ABANDONMENT: retiring an aspiration after two years should be an act that captures what it taught rather than one that archives a folder. | M4 · CLOSED 2026-09-21 BY THE THIRD DOOR, SCHEDULER #10 draining BOB #25's answer to Q1 of DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB (`37367611`): a STATED DEFERRAL in its home design, read there: `BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md`'s front matter (Incomplete sections), §12.2, which defers the PURSUIT RECORD with the intent layer's objects, and its trigger: SCHEDULER schedules the design act when `node tools/status.mjs 12` reads the publication ceremony and the accept surface BUILT, both ABSENT today, or earlier on Bob's word. **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** M4 · open — **SENT 2026-09-21 by SCHEDULER #10 to BOB, Q1 of a group of three (DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB)**, verified at the code on `7a8b81d6`. |
| D-87 | design | 2026-07-30 | **A pattern statement's evidence is cited once and never revisited, so a bias can outlive its justification unremarked.** Bob's reframing: a side-effect of this framework's work should be that evidentiary comments accrue to bias records, measuring the extent to which the bias is JUSTIFIED. The doctrine requires a `pattern` statement to cite evidence and refuses to let one leave draft without a citation; what it lacks is TIME. A statement declared in March citing three instances is a different object by August, either because those three became forty or because they stayed three while sixty went the other way, and nothing notices either. This is the INVERSE of bias debt: bias debt says the lens changed so the analysis owes a re-run, this says the evidence changed so the lens owes a re-examination. The second is the one nobody builds because it is uncomfortable, and a group may keep a statement whose grounds have evaporated provided it says why, but the decay must not go unremarked, since a bias whose grounds have quietly evaporated is undeclared in the way that matters. | M4 · CLOSED 2026-09-21 BY THE THIRD DOOR, SCHEDULER #10 draining BOB #25's answer to Q2 of DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB (`37367611`): a STATED DEFERRAL in its home design, read there: `BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md`'s front matter (Incomplete sections), §13.1, *evidence accruing to a bias statement (D-87) and a statement's MEASURABLE FORM (D-88) are a STATED DEFERRAL with §12's evaluator*; D-87's mechanical half is that measure taken over time, and the `measure-decay` finding stays catalogued with no producer until the intent layer's trigger fires. **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** M4 · open — **SENT 2026-09-21 by SCHEDULER #10 to BOB, Q2 of a group of three (DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB)**, verified at the code on `7a8b81d6`. |
| D-88 | design | 2026-07-30 | **A bias statement should be able to carry a MEASURABLE FORM, and it is the same construct as an objective's satisfaction condition.** Framework §13.1. Most pattern statements are not mechanically measurable and must stay analysis; some are, and those should say so, with the measure expressed in the framework's own vocabulary. Three properties keep it honest and each is easy to lose. The measure NEVER EDITS the statement: text and justification stay a member's authored act and the standing measure is a derived dated attachment, because a machine that could rewrite doctrine would be worse than the problem it solves. The scope is REGISTRY-DEFINED and never hand-picked, since a cherry-picked denominator would let a group manufacture authority for a bias, which is the distortion the malformedness rule fights arriving by the back door. And it is reported WHICHEVER WAY IT CUTS, which is the case where invariant 7 actually costs something, because the value of the feature is entirely in the measures that refute. Build it once with §12's satisfaction-condition evaluator. | M4 · CLOSED 2026-09-21 BY THE THIRD DOOR, SCHEDULER #10 draining BOB #25's answer to Q2 of DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB (`37367611`): a STATED DEFERRAL in its home design, read there: `BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md`'s front matter (Incomplete sections), §13.1: a measurable form is the same construct as a satisfaction condition and is built once, with it, under the intent layer's trigger (`node tools/status.mjs 12` reads the publication ceremony and the accept surface BUILT, or Bob's word). **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** M4 · open — **SENT 2026-09-21 by SCHEDULER #10 to BOB, Q2 of a group of three (DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB)**, verified at the code on `7a8b81d6`. |
| D-120 | design | 2026-07-31 | **Member-driven capture for egress diversity is named nowhere in the ledger.** Raised by DEC-1's enactment: with the allowlist arm closed (D-94), admission on the plane's own egress rests on the CivicOS UA not yet being recognised — not a durable position. The mitigation that SCALES with the project (unlike an allowlist request, one ask per instance forever) is capturing through MEMBERS' own networks: diverse egress that does not all present as one recognisable origin. Needs a design for how a member contributes a capture and how its provenance/grade is recorded (a member's first-party fetch is still evidence at capture time), and how it composes with the archive fallback (CAP-3) as the two scaling resilience mechanisms. | M1 · CLOSED 2026-09-21 BY THE THIRD DOOR, SCHEDULER #10 draining BOB #25's answer to Q3 of DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB (`37367611`): a STATED DEFERRAL in its home design, read there: `SOURCE-ACCESS.md` item 2, *A STATED DEFERRAL, 2026-09-21 (BOB #25, SCHEDULER #10's Q3, D-120), behind item 1*, the archive fallback, which is BUILT; its trigger is named there: a monitored public document the record cannot hold because the source refuses the resilience mechanism. **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** M1 · open (DEC-1) — member-driven egress diversity; placed under M1 in MILESTONES, sibling to CAP-3 as a scaling capture-resilience mechanism — **SENT 2026-09-21 by SCHEDULER #10 to BOB, Q3 of a group of three (DELEGATION 2026-09-21 SCHEDULER (#10) -> BOB)**, verified at the code on `7a8b81d6`. |
| D-82 | design | 2026-07-30 | **An assistant-surfaced focus must be visually distinguishable, and no surface does it.** RULED by Bob: the appearance of an assistant-surfaced focus should communicate that it is one. The record already carries the distinction once D-78 is fixed; this is the display half. The framing matters for how it is built: this is NOT a discount applied to the question, since a good question stands on its merits whoever asked it. What a member needs to know is that nobody has yet judged it worth asking, which is exactly the difference between a machine noticing a pattern and a member deciding it matters, and the marking is what lets them give it the reading it deserves rather than assuming a colleague already thought it through. Applies to the focus list, the review queue, and anywhere a focus appears beside a project. | M8 · CLOSED 2026-09-21 AS A DEBT ROW by LED-7 batch S10-1 (SCHEDULER #10) — PLACED as a BACKLOG task under its own id, D-82 (owner UI, M8), directly after UI-73, the same member-surface class. | D-380 | defect | 2026-09-16 | **THE BATTERY'S FLEET SKIP MESSAGE TELLS `agent-worker` TO RUN A COMMAND THAT EXITS `EUSAGE`, AND TELLS IT TO ADOPT THE IDIOM IT ALREADY IMPLEMENTS — AND UNDERNEATH IT THE FLEET IS RUNNING TWO RESOLUTION IDIOMS, ONE OF WHICH GOES DARK ON A FRESH WORKTREE.** Found by FLEET #1's stand-up, driving the named measurement in its brief rather than reasoning about it; every figure is **M-31**. **THE HALF THAT IS A DEFECT TODAY.** `bio-plane/scripts/battery.mjs`'s `fleetDepSkip` composes its remediation from the member's OWN directory plus a hardcoded exemplar: *run `npm ci` in <member>/, or have the suite resolve it from the plane's install as agent-worker's does*. For `agent-worker` **both halves are wrong**: `npm ci` there exits **`npm error code EUSAGE`** and creates nothing, because a package with no dependencies has no `package-lock.json` (the four-manifest table in M-31 §1) — and the second half names that member's own file as the exemplar to copy, which it has been since FL-2. **The instruction that works is `npm ci` in `bio-plane/`, which no version of the message names.** The same message is CORRECT for `ocr-worker`, so this is a per-member question rather than a wording fix. **THE HALF THAT IS THE REAL FINDING.** `agent-worker` and `pdf-worker` resolve `miniflare` through an explicit two-step loader (bare `import`, then `createRequire(bio-plane/package.json).resolve`) and therefore RUN with their own directory empty — MEASURED at **5/5 green, 628 assertions, `agent-worker/node_modules` absent, plane installed**. `ocr-worker/test/ocr-worker.test.mjs:46` imports `miniflare` bare with no fallback and is **DARK** in the same condition (`0 member(s) actually RAN · DARK: ocr-worker`). **The comment above `fleetDepSkip` asserts "THIS PATH IS NOW UNREACHABLE FOR EVERY MEMBER THAT EXISTS" on the premise that "the plane's install is present wherever this battery can run at all" — REPRODUCED FALSE 2026-09-16** on a pristine worktree: the battery carries no dependencies, so it runs with nothing installed, and four `agent-worker` suites went dark at **exit 0**. That comment PREDICTED its own falsification (it keeps the branch as *the landing pad for the NEXT member, which will be written before it has adopted the idiom*) and `ocr-worker` is that member; the landing pad worked, and nobody adopted the idiom afterwards. **COSTS WHILE OPEN:** on any fresh worktree `ocr-worker` is silently unrun while the battery reports **green and exits 0** — the residual the comment itself names as *the fleet's to weigh*, now with a receipt; and a session that meets the skip and obeys the message gets an error for one member, or copies a file that needs no copying. It is the trap `CLAUDE.md` already carries one package further out (*a baseline measured before `npm ci` is a wrong number carrying full confidence*), arriving as a REMEDIATION that cannot be performed. **CLOSING IT TAKES** two edits in two areas, NEITHER taken by the finder and both routed rather than left as prose: (1) **FLEET's ground** — give `ocr-worker`'s suite the same two-step loader, which M-31 §6 establishes is sufficient because its only bare import is `miniflare` (the engine's wasm and language model are COMMITTED ASSETS, not `node_modules` reads), leaving the member's own install necessary for its BUILD alone; (2) **the battery owner's ground** — correct the remediation to name the resolution path per member and correct the unreachability comment, DELEGATED in `CLAIMS.md` 2026-09-16 (FLEET → whoever owns `battery.mjs`), because FL-9 set FLEET's own precedent that the runner is not this area's ground. The two are COUPLED and the delegation says so: if (1) lands, the exemplar becomes accurate for all three members; if it does not, the message must name the path per member. | M0 · CLOSED 2026-09-21 AS A DEBT ROW by LED-7 batch S10-1 (SCHEDULER #10) — PLACED as a BACKLOG task under its own id, D-380 (owner FLEET, with the battery's owner, M0), directly after M0-94, in the instrument cluster. Verified before placing, on `ab34197b`: `ocr-worker/test/ocr-worker.test.mjs` imports `miniflare` bare, and `fleetDepSkip` and the comment above it in `bio-plane/scripts/battery.mjs` are unchanged. **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** M0 · FLEET + the battery owner · **open, disposition OPEN-WITH-A-NAMED-EDIT rather than accepted** — nothing to decide and nothing to design; one loader to copy and one message to correct, both measured, neither taken because activation and routing are the LEAD's and this was a stand-up. **The condition is bounded meanwhile**: the `fleet:` line names a dark member on every run (`DARK: <name>`), so the hole is reported — what is not bounded is that the headline and the exit status both still read healthy. |
| D-288 | defect | 2026-08-10 | **EVERY WORKER'S OUTPUT LIVES ON A LOCAL-ONLY BRANCH, SO "THE REPOSITORY IS THE CHANNEL" IS FALSE ONE LEVEL BELOW WHERE IT IS ENFORCED.** Measured 2026-08-10 by session BOB and confirmed by CONDUCT with `git ls-remote`: **137 local `worktree-agent-*` branches, ZERO on the remote** — including the three green unmerged items the CONDUCT handoff instructs the next session to merge FIRST. **`CLAUDE.md`'s rule is that a change is made when it is COMMITTED AND PUSHED, and `plancheck` enforces exactly that — for `main` and for planning surfaces. Nothing enforces it for a WORKER BRANCH**, which is where every item's actual code sits between the worker reporting and CONDUCT merging, a window that has repeatedly lasted a day or more. **THE EXPOSURE IS TOTAL AND SILENT: a pruned worktree, a lost clone, or a successor working from a fresh checkout, and the work is gone — and the handoff naming those branches would read as a claim about branches that cannot be found**, which is worse than no claim. This is the project's most-repeated rule failing at the one altitude it was never applied to. **NOT A HYPOTHETICAL: `WORKER.md` tells workers `Do not push. Do not merge. CONDUCT integrates.`** — so the instruction that keeps `main` safe is also the instruction that strands the output, and neither half was written with the other in view. **PARTIALLY REMEDIATED, and the rest deliberately not:** BOB pushed the three branches the handoff names (`worktree-agent-a61e489de171ae6c5` D-254 at `9e24ef6`, `worktree-agent-a9e7e017d06799858` UI-43 at `9706d19`, `worktree-agent-aafee89563a3f2d42` D-270 at `484ed35`), each verified from the remote by sha; **they are BACKUPS, not merge requests, and the integration order is unchanged.** The other 134 were left alone — most are long-merged and pushing them would make the remote's branch list useless as a signal, which is its own defect. WHAT CLOSING IT TAKES: a decision first, and it is not a worker's — either (a) **`WORKER.md` gains "push your branch"**, cheapest and it makes the remote's branch list noisy in exactly the way the 134 warn about, so it needs a pruning rule in the same turn; or (b) **CONDUCT pushes a branch when a worker REPORTS**, which keeps the remote clean and puts the durability at the one place that already knows the item is done — but leaves the window between the worker's last commit and its report unprotected; or (c) a `plancheck` warning naming any local `worktree-agent-*` branch that is neither merged nor on the remote, which detects rather than prevents and is the shape this project usually prefers **because it cannot go silent**. Relation: `CLAUDE.md`'s channel rule, `WORKER.md`'s do-not-push, PARALLELISM.md/DEC-3, D-263 and D-264 (both merges that DROPPED content, i.e. the same altitude one step later). | M0 · **CLOSED IN FULL 2026-09-17: item 3, prune-on-merge, was BUILT by M0-49 (`4b7f05e8`, merged `4ce4d9e5` by CONDUCT #2), which this cell called unbuilt; archived 2026-09-21 by BOB #25.** **CLOSED 2026-09-16 by BOB #12, AND THE CLOSURE WAS DRIVEN RATHER THAN READ OFF A GREEN RUN.** The condition I set was *the row is not closed until item 2 lands*, and item 2 landed at `f1c8d870`. **I drove the arm on my own worktree — the only one I may perturb — rather than trusting M0-48's fixtures or CONDUCT's report:** dirtied it, the arm NAMED it (*1 modified, 1 untracked; nothing committed past origin/main -> COMMIT FIRST, no push can help*); cleaned it, the arm went QUIET. **Named -> acted -> quiet is the shape that makes a conditioned assertion believable, and a green run never is.** My own branch has NO ref on origin, so that test also incidentally drove the null-sha path M0-48 found in itself — the one where a unit with no remote ref threw from inside a WARN and killed `plancheck`, removing every check after it; it did not throw, and every later note printed. **AND IT PAID FOR ITSELF ON ITS FIRST BARE RUN, on work nobody went looking for:** it named `objective-elgamal-295877`, FLEET's worktree, 1 commit on no remote — **a session had ENDED and taken a measurement with it**, and nothing in the estate would have said so an hour earlier. Now `280f8a5f` on origin, verified. **WHAT THE CLOSURE DOES NOT CLAIM, stated because the distinction is the whole discipline: the arm closes the DURABILITY gap, not the INTEGRATION one.** FLEET's commit is safe and is NOT on `main`, and the D-380 it raises is NOT in this file, because the row it was written on has not merged. **Rescued is not landed.** **ITEM 3 (prune-on-merge) IS NOT BUILT and does NOT hold this row open** — it maintains a signal rather than protecting work, and a merged branch the arm judges reads `clear` rather than as noise, so its absence costs hygiene and not exposure. Carried as its own row. **The original ruling and its reasoning follow, kept whole because the argument is why the shape is what it is.** THE DECISION WAS RULED 2026-09-16 by BOB #12 (mechanism, BOB's): shapes (a) AND (c), plus a PRUNING rule that turns (a)'s cost into a signal. Not (b) alone.** The row waited five weeks for a decision and then COST SOMETHING: REC-91 finished, committed and released on `worktree-agent-aabecaced11e00db1`, its integrator was stood down before merging, and the work reached nobody — this row's exposure, realised exactly as written. **(a) `WORKER.md` gains PUSH YOUR BRANCH BEFORE YOU REPORT**, because the worker is the only actor GUARANTEED to be alive at the moment the commits exist; every other shape rests durability on a second session surviving to act. **(c) `plancheck` WARNS on any local `worktree-agent-*` branch neither merged into `origin/main` nor present on the remote** — detection inside the loop everyone already runs, which cannot go silent, and which catches what (a) misses (a worker that dies mid-item, before reporting). **PRUNING: CONDUCT deletes the remote branch when it merges the item**, which is what makes (a) affordable and INVERTS the cost this row feared — with pruning, a `worktree-agent-*` branch on the remote MEANS UNINTEGRATED WORK, so the branch list becomes a worklist rather than noise. The 134 historical local branches are NOT retroactively pushed; they are what this row says they are, mostly long-merged. **(b) is declined with its reason recorded so it is not re-argued:** it keeps the remote cleanest and would probably have saved REC-91, but it rests every item's durability on a second session performing an act between the worker's report and its own stand-down — and `CLAUDE.md` already names that class, a stand-down being a voluntary act by a session that might not survive to perform it. Decomposed to CONDUCT through the BOB INBOX as three items the same day. **TRACKED HERE AND NOT ONLY IN THE INBOX, BECAUSE CONDUCT DELETES AN INBOX ENTRY WHEN IT DRAINS IT** — a record of what remains that lives only in the channel disappears at the moment the channel is read, which is the same altitude mistake as the one this row describes. **ITEM 1 LANDED 2026-09-16 by BOB** (`WORKER.md`: push your own branch, verify from the remote, lead the report with the pushed sha) — the instruction that stranded REC-91 is gone. [The condition written here on 2026-09-16 — that items 2 and 3 were still to land and the row stayed open until item 2 did — is quoted verbatim in CLAIMS.md, DELEGATION 2026-09-16 M0 (M0-48) -> BOB; reworded here by BOB #25 so the archived row declares no residue it no longer has.] Item 1 PREVENTS; item 2 DETECTS, and it is the only thing that will ever tell anyone a branch was stranded. **The warning this row is entitled to give about itself: it sat `open` for five weeks WITH A DISPOSITION, and `plancheck` only fails on an open row with NONE — so the estate's own watchdog was satisfied for the whole period the exposure was total. A disposition is not a schedule.** BOB verifies item 2's landing against the tree and reports closure; Bob is not to be the one who chases it. **ITEM 1 IS MEASURED WORKING, 2026-09-16, and not merely landed:** `git ls-remote` counts **16 `worktree-agent-*` branches on origin against 3 that morning**, six dated the 16th, one worker's own commit subject reading *"release the claim, with the push verified from the remote"* — `WORKER.md`'s new wording, obeyed. **AND THE WINDOW THIS ROW NAMED AS SHAPE (b)'s OBJECTION IS CLOSING FROM AN UNEXPECTED DIRECTION:** the row argued (b) *"leaves the window between the worker's last commit and its report unprotected"*, and on the same day CONDUCT measured that a worker which STOPPED with work UNCOMMITTED — this row's own failure shape forming a second time — **was RESUMED BY A MESSAGE and then committed, pushed and verified.** Item 1 protects work ONCE COMMITTED; the subagent channel protects work that is NOT YET committed. **They cover different failures and together cover more than this row's three shapes contemplated.** Recorded in `kickoffs/BOB.md` rule 6 with what is still UNestablished — a resume is not a correction — because collapsing those is how a premise gets replaced by its opposite and stays just as unchecked. **A THIRD WINDOW EXISTS AND THIS ROW'S THREE SHAPES DID NOT CONTEMPLATE IT — measured 2026-09-16 as M-38, sweeping every worktree rather than the three CONDUCT checked by hand. FOUR live exposures, in THREE shapes: never-pushed, PUSHED-THEN-BEHIND, and committed-nothing-with-11-files-live.** The middle one is new: item 1 protects a worker that pushes ONCE at the end, and REC-100 and M0-40 each pushed in the MIDDLE and committed after, stranding a *"release the claim"* commit — **so the loss would have been the DURABLE REPORT itself.** **AND THE HEADLINE: M0-48, THIS ROW'S OWN ITEM 2, WAS ITSELF A STRANDED WORKER BRANCH AT THE MOMENT OF THE SWEEP** — two commits on no remote, one of them titled *plancheck WARNS on a local worker branch whose commits reach nobody*. Nothing reported it; it was found by looking, which is the argument that put the item in the cohort. **Item 2's arm therefore needs THREE words and not one warning** — the reader's next act differs per shape (push it · push it · ask the worker to COMMIT, which a push cannot do) — **and it must not name a FRESH worktree**, whose tip sits at `origin/main` with nothing committed and no exposure; an arm that cries about a healthy state is tuned out within a day. That over-strictness case is the SAME fact CONDUCT's prune criterion read the opposite way an hour earlier, calling six live workers prunable: a tip at `origin/main` means nothing is committed yet, and two instruments drew opposite wrong conclusions from it. **ITEM 3 (PRUNE-ON-MERGE) IS BUILT, 2026-09-17 by M0-49 — this row's own text above says it "IS NOT BUILT", which was true when written and is corrected here rather than left to be re-derived.** `kickoffs/CONDUCT.md`'s INTEGRATION SEQUENCE now carries it as the last act of step 2: once the merge is pushed AND the push verified, `git push origin --delete <branch>`, confirmed from `git ls-remote`. **The ordering is the safety and it is stated at the site, because this row's own justification is only conditionally true:** *the content is on `origin/main` by definition of having merged it* holds once the merge is ON THE REMOTE and is FALSE in the window before, where a local merge plus a remote delete re-creates this row's exposure with the act meant to close it. **AND THE TWO ACTS ARE KEPT SEPARATE AT THE SITE** — deleting a merged remote branch destroys nothing; REMOVING A WORKTREE can take a live worker's uncommitted tree, so the step disclaims being licence for it. **The prune criterion's own hazard is now stated beside it**: for a worker that has STARTED, ancestry does not fail to distinguish, it positively answers PRUNE — the six-live-workers measurement this row already carries — so the live-list is the entire safety of that act and not a refinement of it. **What it is NOT: a GATE.** Nothing forces the deletion; `strandedwork` warns on work that is stranded and is silent about a branch that lingers after merging. The arms added to `strandedwork.test.mjs` §10 assert the step is PRESENT and IN the sequence, which audits the DOCUMENT and not the behaviour — stated plainly because a discipline described as a gate is exactly the overclaim this estate is built to refuse. |

| D-152 | design | 2026-08-01 | **Image-only PDFs must be OCR'd and investigated, and OCR text must never be indistinguishable from text the publisher wrote.** RULED 2026-08-01 (DEC-4), overturning the recommendation that scanned PDFs stay honestly unreadable. Bob: *"Some PDFs will be released in the public record as image only. The textual contents of these images need to be extracted and investigated for meaningful content."* The refused argument was a volume argument — ~14% of a 14-document sample, skewing to design and scan artifacts — and coverage is not a volume question: a class the record can capture and never read is a hole, and it does not have to be large, only non-empty. The class is also not randomly assigned; scanned-only is what a signed order, an exhibit, a handwritten annotation and a deliberately-imaged release all look like. *"Investigated for meaningful content"* puts OCR output on the READING path (FW-15's L2→L3 wire), not merely in a text field, or the class is captured, readable and still ungraphed. **THE PROVENANCE RULE IS STRUCTURAL AND IS THE THING THAT COULD TURN THIS CAPABILITY INTO AN OVERCLAIMING DEFECT:** extracted text carries `text_source: 'layer'`; OCR carries `text_source: 'ocr'` with engine, version and per-region confidence, and they are distinguishable everywhere the text is shown, cited or indexed. A basis leg resting on OCR'd text carries its IMAGE REGION (page + rect) so a reader checks the claim against the pixels rather than against our transcription — the same discipline as answering by hash. OCR never raises a capture grade, and a low-confidence region reads `undetermined` rather than a best guess, which is the mojibake rule one layer up. **MEASUREMENT GATES DESIGN:** whether OCR runs in-plane at all is unknown — a WASM engine's bundle size against the Worker limit and its CPU against the isolate ceiling (D-36, D-56) — with the pdf-worker fleet member (I6) the first fallback and an external service the second. Nothing is designed before that number exists. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M2 · open — NARROWED 2026-09-22 by LED-7 batch S11-1 (SCHEDULER #11) to ONE unbuilt trace, and SENT to BOB, Q1 of a group of four (DELEGATION 2026-09-22 SCHEDULER (#11) -> BOB). BUILT, verified on `06832aff`: CPDF-9 and CPDF-10 are done (`698a07b`). OCR runs in the `ocr-worker` fleet member; its text carries a transcription CHAIN, never a label (`textchain.mjs` `checkChain`: `'ocr'` alone is refused, C-35.1, and so is an engine left unnamed, C-35.5); every OCR region carries a page-and-rect anchor or is dropped (`checkAnchor`, C-35.9); a region below the floor reads undetermined; fidelity bounds a leg's capture grade (C-2.8, REC-88); attestation is a member's act scoped to its extent. NOT BUILT: DEC-4's *an OCR citation carries its image region* at the LEG. A basis leg may carry `extent_rect` on the `pdf-page` arm, and nothing refuses one resting on OCR'd text that names none; it is only capped, inheriting no region's attestation. **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** M2 - open · CPDF-9 (measure), CPDF-10 (build) | M2 · CLOSED 2026-09-22 IN FACT, ruled by BOB #26 (the BOB INBOX entry of 2026-09-22, drained by SCHEDULER #12): DEC-4's *an OCR citation carries its image region* is met at the leg by a CAP, never a refusal (`BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md` §14.4). | D-125 | gap | 2026-08-01 | **A member has no notification preferences, and DEC-10's ruling requires them.** RULED 2026-08-01: an overdue progression stage notifies the member who AUTHORED the connection between that instance and a Focus or Project, and the notification must offer three things -- remind me again at a further increment overdue, stop notifying me about this one, stop notifying me about that GROUP. The plane has no state for any of it: `tasks` carries `open`/`forwarded`/`resolved` and nothing per-member and nothing with a snooze clock. Needed: a per-member preference keyed on the thing muted, where the mute key is expressible on the axes the CONNECTION was made on (progression type, body, or Focus/Project) so a member mutes what they mean rather than a bucket the system chose; a snooze whose increment is THE STAGE'S OWN DECLARED INTERVAL rather than a new constant, since the expectation already carries the number and a second one would drift from it. **THE HAZARD, and it is the one to build against first: MUTING IS PERSONAL, DISMISSING IS A RECORD ACT, and they must never be one control.** If 'stop notifying me' also dismisses the finding, one member's inbox hygiene erases a finding for the whole group -- which is the silent disappearance the discovery-loop doctrine forbids (D-79, Step 8b). The finding stands; only that member's notification stops. NOTE the channel itself already exists: D-98's task inbox is the in-app notification channel, so D-52 ('there is no notification channel in this system at all', raised for export notification) is substantially answered by the same mechanism -- one channel, three consumers, which is the D-86 lesson applied rather than a second scheduler's worth of bugs. AMENDED 2026-08-01 (DEC-10 amendment): notifications AGGREGATE PER CASE, so the preference state is needed at TWO levels, not one -- per item and per (member, Focus/Project) group. The group entry is STANDING and ACCUMULATING rather than a stream, which removes any digest-cadence machinery: one live entry per (member, case) while it holds unhandled events, re-notifying only on a snooze increment or when something new lands after the member last looked. AND THE GROUP MUTE CARRIES ITS OWN HAZARD: it must be scoped to the KINDS PRESENT WHEN IT WAS MADE, so a genuinely new kind of event on that case surfaces again -- otherwise 'mute this case' is a permanent blindfold, which is the same silent-disappearance failure as conflating mute with dismiss, one level up. Grouping itself needs no new stored relation: `tasks.refers_to` names a bundle and the case comes from the citation edge that already exists, so the group is DERIVED from the authored connection rather than duplicated beside it. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M4 - open; the inbox half M8 — NARROWED 2026-09-22 by LED-7 batch S11-1 (SCHEDULER #11) to DEC-10's own subject, and SENT to BOB, Q3 of a group of four (DELEGATION 2026-09-22 SCHEDULER (#11) -> BOB). BUILT, verified on `06832aff` (REC-21 and P-87): `queue_state` per member and case; muting is personal and never dismisses; a mute holds the kinds present when it was made, so a new kind still reaches the member; the aggregation key is the case (DEC-10's amendment); a snooze is the member's own instant with no instance constant, and a finding that keeps coming due re-notifies at its stage's declared `within_interval`. NOT BUILT: DEC-10's (b) and (c) for the overdue FINDING. `muted_kinds` admits CONDITION kinds only, and a FINDING is refused `KIND_NOT_PERSONAL` (`queuestate.mjs` `MUTE_REFUSAL_DETAIL`), where DEC-10 rules a personal stop whose finding stands. **Prior disposition, kept verbatim:** M4 - open; the inbox half M8 | M8 · CLOSED 2026-09-22 AS A DEBT ROW by LED-7 (SCHEDULER #12) — PLACED as a BACKLOG task under its own id, D-125 (owner RECORD, M8), last of the M8 corrections after D-82, ruled by BOB #26 (`NOTIFICATIONS.md` "MARKED AS HANDLED"). | D-148 | design | 2026-08-01 | **A fee quote is EVIDENCE about a public body, and the design treats it as an administrative obstacle.** Sourced examples: Colorado's Secretary of State quoted **$2.1M** (69,736 projected labor hours) for election contracts; Texas DCJ quoted **$1,132,024.30** for records the state PREA office supplied for **$551.39** — **a 2,000x spread for identical records**; Nevada DETR quoted $650,000, then **dropped a $1,083 charge entirely and released the records free once challenged**, which is direct evidence the quote was not cost-based; DoD quoted **~$660 million** [MuckRock survey Dec 2023; Las Vegas Review-Journal Sept 2023; Techdirt — press and NGO sources, labelled as such]. Against that, the federal fee system collects **0.34-0.38% of what FOIA costs to run**, about **$1.54 per request**, and the State Department collected **$0.00** in FY2024 [government's own reported statistics] — **fees are a deterrent instrument, not a cost-recovery one**, and the record should be able to say so with its own captured evidence. Consequence for the design: a fee quote should be a first-class captured artifact with a comparison basis, not correspondence prose. Two identical requests priced 2,000x apart is exactly the shape BIO exists to make checkable, and today it would be a paragraph in a `## Correspondence` heading. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M4 - open — **ROUTED TO BOB 2026-09-19 by LED-7 batch 4 (SCHEDULER #2).** A fee quote treated as evidence about a public body rather than as an obstacle is a DOCTRINE question about what the record holds and asserts — and the row's own figures (a 2,000x spread for identical records; a charge dropped entirely once challenged) are the argument for it. Bears on people outside the project, which is Bob's by `CLAUDE.md` §3. Not placed. **PROVISIONAL STATED 2026-09-20 by BOB #18, because an open decision must never block work (`kickoffs/README.md`) and this row carried none for 50 days:** what RUNS today is that a fee quote is CORRESPONDENCE PROSE under a `## Correspondence` heading — captured, readable, attributable, and NOT comparable; nothing puts two quotes side by side and the record asserts nothing about them. Nothing is blocked by the question staying open: no queue row depends on it. **What the delay COSTS is asymmetric and is the reason to rule it before data accumulates** — quotes captured as prose that later become first-class artifacts need re-extraction and move any citation resting on them, while a modelled artifact later abandoned drops a table and leaves the capture untouched. Carried to Bob 2026-09-20 in the `README.md` decision shape. | M10 · CLOSED 2026-09-22 AS A DEBT ROW by LED-7 (SCHEDULER #13) — PLACED as a BACKLOG task under its own id, D-148 (owner RECORD, M10), after UI-69 with the M10 case path, on Bob's ruling of 2026-09-22 folded into `BIO_Case_Making_v0_1.md` §2 (BOB #26). | D-149 | defect | 2026-08-01 | **The design is jurisdiction-blind, and BIO's own jurisdiction differs from federal FOIA on every axis that matters.** Nothing in the record models WHICH access regime an `action` runs under, and the assumptions differ sharply. Federal: fee-shifting is discretionary ('the court MAY assess... substantially prevailed', 5 USC 552(a)(4)(E)), search and review fees are chargeable by requester class, and plaintiffs substantially prevailed in only **112 of 1,672 lawsuits, 6.7%** [GAO-16-667, the government auditing itself]. **California, which is Oakland and therefore BIO's actual regime**: fee-shifting is MANDATORY ('the court SHALL award court costs and reasonable attorney's fees to the requester', Gov. Code 7923.115(a)); fees are **direct duplication cost only** with no search fee and no review fee (7922.530(a)); and **redaction time is not billable at all** — *National Lawyers Guild v. City of Hayward* (2020) 9 Cal.5th 488 threw out a $2,938.58 invoice for 40.2 hours of bodycam redaction, holding that 'extraction' means retrieving responsive data, not omitting unwanted data. So in California 'it will take us 200 hours to redact this' cannot lawfully become a bill — and a design carrying federal assumptions would model a cost that does not exist here and miss a violation when one is charged. A pending 2026 California bill would loosen the fee rules [CalMatters, Mar 2026]; flag as pending, not law. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M4 - open — **ROUTED TO BOB 2026-09-19 by LED-7 batch 4 (SCHEDULER #2), and it is D-148's sibling — rule them together.** The record models no access regime, while BIO's actual jurisdiction (California) differs from federal FOIA on every axis the row lists: fee-shifting MANDATORY not discretionary, direct duplication cost only, redaction time not billable. Deciding whether an `action` carries its regime is design, not a fix. Also bears on people outside the project. **PROVISIONAL STATED 2026-09-20 by BOB #18 (same reason as D-148, its sibling):** what RUNS today is that an `action` carries NO access regime and the design's implicit assumptions are federal-FOIA ones, which are wrong for Oakland on every axis this row lists. Nothing is blocked: no queue row depends on it. **The cost of waiting is NOT symmetric with D-148's** — nothing is being captured into a wrong shape, so no re-extraction accrues; the exposure is that a member could read a federal assumption as their own regime. **And one live fact makes part of this self-resolving: the row's own pending-2026-bill flag means any ENCODED rule would go stale while a CITATION would not** — which is the shape of the recommendation carried to Bob 2026-09-20. | M10 · CLOSED 2026-09-22 AS A DEBT ROW by LED-7 (SCHEDULER #13) — PLACED as a BACKLOG task under its own id, D-149 (owner RECORD, M10), directly after D-148, on Bob's ruling of 2026-09-22 folded into `BIO_Case_Making_v0_1.md` §2 (BOB #26). | D-153 | design | 2026-08-01 | **`research/AUDIENCES.md` H4 and H6 are not hazards to be avoided; they are the required workflow — corrected by external evidence, and the pass is NOT edited.** RULED 2026-08-01 (DEC-13). AUDIENCES §7 runs `RATIFY -> CASE -> rendering -> action -> consequence` and §9 lists the pre-publication release of material among its HAZARDS — H4 (*the low-threshold rendering that escapes*) and H6 (*the embargo*). The practice literature says the ordering is inverted, and the sources are professional standards rather than opinion: the SPJ code (*"diligently seek out subjects of news stories to give them the opportunity to respond to allegations of wrongdoing"*); GAGAS/Yellow Book, where obtaining the views of responsible officials is a REPORTING REQUIREMENT and GAO's own protocols give an agency **7 to 30 calendar days** on a draft report, expect a single position including the rationale for any disagreement, and print the response beside the finding; and the Columbia Journalism School review of Rolling Stone's *A Rape on Campus*, which identified a comment request made **without specifics** as the central failure. So an unratified rendering carrying specific claims must be able to leave the building, addressed to the subject, under a deadline, before anything is ratified — which is exactly AUDIENCES row 14's *"delivery with no bucket"*, and the outside evidence says that row is the MAIN PATH for any case that names anybody rather than an edge case. **THE PASS IS DELIBERATELY NOT EDITED** — a research pass is a record of what it concluded, and correcting it in place would rewrite history and hide that the evidence arrived later and overturned it; same treatment `RECONCILED.md` gave the storyboard files, with the correction living in a live surface and a pointer going on the pass. What Bob's ruling adds beyond the sources: the GATE is not the contact but the group's DECLARED, JUSTIFIED POSITION on it, carried inside the case as declared bias — which is what makes a tipping-off refusal legitimate and visible instead of quiet. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M10 - open · REC-14, REC-24, UI-17 | M10 · CLOSED 2026-09-22 IN FACT by LED-7 batch S13-1 (SCHEDULER #13): DEC-13's ruled workflow is on the tree. C-2.10's `request_for_comment` arm (`bio-checks.mjs`) refuses a comment request naming zero inquiries (the specifics the Columbia review found missing) and requires its response window as a clock entry; the correspondence ledger records a non-response dated by when the reply was due; `BIO_Publication_v0_1.md` §3 rule 6 folds the right of reply as the group's declaration in its recorded bias, and §4's table reads it RULED and BUILT (verified on `8ec66c57`). REC-14 and REC-24 are done; UI-17, the ceremony, stays blocked in `BACKLOG.md` (DEC-33). |
| D-156 | design | 2026-08-02 | **"Audience" names two different things in this project, and they have different machinery attached.** Named by Bob 2026-08-02 (DEC-17 addendum): in requirements gathering the word refers to **user types** — the people who use BIO; when referring to a published case it means **the readers and consumers of that case** — elements of the public who use the information, analysis and conclusions it contains. VERIFIED in the corpus: `research/AUDIENCES.md` enumerates *"eight audiences"* mixing both senses in one list — an administrator or a lawyer READING A PUBLISHED CASE is the second sense, while *"the publisher's own future members"* and the primary archetype (*a member of a community accountability group*, from `JOURNEY-PRIMARY.md`) are BIO USERS and are the first; the word `archetype` occurs twice in the entire file. **THE CONFLATION IS WHY THAT FILE'S §5 READS AS A WARNING RATHER THAN A DEFINITION.** Its hard constraint — *"a per-audience relaxation is a structural prior by role… if administrators need a lower threshold, that is a threshold on a RENDERING, and it must never be a threshold on RATIFICATION"* — is correct and unchanged, but it had to be written defensively because "administrator" sat in a list mixing a user type with a case reader, which makes a per-role gate look like a natural extension of the list. Separated, the rule is definitional: **renderings and thresholds vary by AUDIENCE; capabilities, journeys and surfaces vary by USER TYPE; neither ever reaches ratification.** DISAMBIGUATION ADOPTED: `AUDIENCE` is reserved for readers/consumers of a published case; the requirements sense is `USER TYPE`, or `ARCHETYPE` where a document already uses that word. This is the D-68 / D-8 vocabulary-drift class caught BEFORE it reached the code — no surface, table or op names either concept today, so the correction costs documents and no migration, which is the whole reason to pay it now. The affected documents are `research/AUDIENCES.md`, `research/JOURNEY-PRIMARY.md`, `UI-PLAN.md` and `research/CAPABILITIES.md`; the research passes take a pointer rather than an edit (D-153's reasoning). **AMENDED 2026-08-02 by Bob, and the correction is larger than the vocabulary split:** the USER TYPE list is not one archetype. Bob: the community-accountability-group member is the PRIMARY archetype, but user types also include *"journalists, lawyers, city administrators, an oversight body, and any other role that involves collecting evidence, investigating, and any other group interested in improving government using the capabilities and processes of the CivicOS."* **The consequence D-156 did not see: the same person can be BOTH.** A lawyer may USE an instance to build a case and may also READ someone else's published case — the two senses are not two populations of people, they are two RELATIONSHIPS to a case, and which one applies depends on the case, not on the person. That is why the machinery differs (capabilities and surfaces versus renderings and thresholds) and why neither ever reaches ratification. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M10 - open · a documentation pass, no code | M10 · CLOSED 2026-09-22 IN FACT by LED-7 batch S13-1 (SCHEDULER #13): the documentation pass is made. `BIO_Publication_v0_1.md` §6 states that an audience is a READER of a published case, distinct from the requirements' user types and archetypes, though one person may be both, and cites this row (verified on `8ec66c57`); no code was ever in scope. |

| D-150 | design | 2026-08-01 | **The completeness claim is VALIDATED by the strongest external evidence in the study — and the same evidence says an authored field is necessary and not sufficient.** From `PROBLEM-DOMAIN.md`: concealing exculpatory evidence appears in **44% of exonerations involving official misconduct**; Nifong scoped a report to exclude; Fujitsu edited out references to known defects; South Yorkshire amended 116 statements. **Every retained fact was true in every one of those cases** — which is precisely the failure the completeness claim targets and the reason a case is not merely a published finding. That is the clearest validation any BIO construct has received from outside the repo. BUT THE SAME SOURCES CARRY A CORRECTION: in both the SBI and Rolling Stone reviews the catch was **a second person with standing**, not a form field — and in each the person who found the gap was overruled inside the hierarchy. So the authored exclusion statement is necessary and insufficient, and the design should say so rather than let the field stand in for the check. What that implies is not settled here: candidates are a second-member acknowledgement on the exclusion statement before publication, or making the statement the thing a reviewer is specifically asked to attack rather than one field among many. Do not build the field and call the problem solved. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M10 - open · REC-14 (disposition corrected 2026-08-01 by session BOB: this row was written `M6` when the ladder had no rung above M8, and M6 is *the record can be left, mirrored and outlived* — nothing to do with a completeness claim at publication. | D-147 | design | 2026-08-01 | **The `action` object models a records request as one round trip, and the real lifecycle has at least four decision points with their own clocks and outcomes.** `action` runs `planned -> active -> awaiting_response -> resolved / abandoned`, so `awaiting_response` collapses everything between asking and getting. The externally-sourced lifecycle (see `PROBLEM-DOMAIN.md`) has distinct stages that each produce a different fact about the body: the FEE ESTIMATE, the FEE-WAIVER decision, PARTIAL PRODUCTION, the ADMINISTRATIVE APPEAL, and litigation. Each has its own clock and its own outcome vocabulary, and collapsing them loses precisely what an accountability record wants. Federal appeal adjudication averaged 96.91 days in FY2024 [DOJ OIP, government's own statistics] and appeal outcomes vary by an order of magnitude between agencies — complete reversal 9.3% at State, 17.6% at DOJ, 31.4% at DHS [computed from the three agencies' own FY2024 annual reports] — so **a design must not assume a single reversal rate or a single response shape**. This is D-128's declared-versus-actual flow with real numbers attached: the declared flow is the statutory timeline, the actual flow is what these figures describe, and the delta is the finding. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M4 - open | M10 · CLOSED 2026-09-22 AS A DEBT ROW by LED-7 (SCHEDULER #14) — PLACED as a BACKLOG task under its own id, D-147 (owner RECORD, M10), directly after D-149, depends-on D-148 and D-149, on BOB #27's design in `BIO_Case_Making_v0_1.md` §2 (the records-request lifecycle). Verified before placing, on `17c0cab1`: `action` runs planned, active, awaiting_response, resolved or abandoned, and no entry grammar carries a later stage. |
| D-128 | design | 2026-08-01 | **The analytic product of progressions is the DELTA between declared and observed flow, and nothing names it as such.** Bob, 2026-08-01: "Obligations are the flows (edges) that go on in a living civic system. Understanding those flows, evolving that understanding, and differentiating between how things are supposed to flow and how they really flow (and the implications of those differences)." This corrects how the OBLIGATION class was first written in NOTIFICATIONS.md — as "a named person must act for the record to proceed", which is a MEMBER TASK and conflated two objects. The CIVIC obligation (the body's declared duty: minutes within N days, an award follows a solicitation) is the primary thing and is a model of how the institution is SUPPOSED to work; the member task is downstream plumbing that frequently arises BECAUSE a civic obligation was observed unmet. Naming them one thing models our inbox and calls it the institution. WHAT FOLLOWS: a progression definition is the DECLARED flow, its instances are the OBSERVED flow, and the divergence has three shapes — missing predecessor (D-73's sharper case), overdue successor (DEC-10), and out-of-order stage (named here for the first time; nothing models it). The delta is not a by-product of progressions, it is the reason to have them, and it needs somewhere to live: a flow model per institution that EVOLVES as understanding does, since a group's picture of how a body is supposed to work is itself a claim that gets refined and should carry its own basis. D-74's shared-identifier measurement is what makes cross-system flows observable at better than Grade C. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M4 · open | M4 · CLOSED 2026-09-22 AS A DEBT ROW by LED-7 (SCHEDULER #14) — PLACED as a BACKLOG task under its own id, D-128 (owner FRAMEWORK, M4), directly after the honesty batch (D-169, D-171, D-179), a correction to built work, on BOB #27's ruling in `BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md` §8.2 (the declared flow, and its revisions). Verified before placing, on `17c0cab1`: `op=progressiondefine` writes `progression_defs` ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE and rewrites the stages (`store.mjs`); the out-of-order shape stays deferred with §8.2's trigger. |
| D-159 | design | 2026-08-02 | **An ungraded leg now costs a conclusion nothing, which makes leaving one ungraded the cheapest way to keep an inconvenient leg out of the reckoning.** Created by DEC-18: an ungraded leg is INERT — it does not contribute, does not floor and does not unrate — so a member holding a leg that cuts against their conclusion can decline to grade it and the conclusion stands on its load-bearing legs. This is a laundering path in the OPPOSITE direction from the one R1 closed, and it is named here rather than left for a build session to discover. **Three defences already stand and a fourth should not be invented before use shows one is needed:** every ungraded leg is NAMED, on the case and in the index row (DEC-18's plural clause); the completeness statement is authored, unprefilled and must say what the case leaves out; and invariant 7 makes a leg that cuts against travel the same path as one that supports. **What would settle it is USE, not argument** — whether real groups leave cuts-against legs ungraded is observable once M9/M10 ship, and inventing a counterweight now would be the same mistake as a global strength floor (§4 Q2). **RE-READ 2026-08-08 by PL-14 / IS-7 against a machine shaping legs at volume (the re-reading QUEUE IS-7 asked for), OUTCOME: THE RULE IS UNCHANGED AND NO FOURTH DEFENCE IS OWED — but ONE THING DID CHANGE and it is not the rule.** (a) **THE MACHINE HOLDS NO LEVER, and that is now enforced rather than intended.** D-159's hazard is a CHOICE — a member declining to grade a leg that cuts against them. A machine has no such choice: §5 rules that *"the grades arrive from the record"* and PL-14 enforces it at the READ, taking every effective grade from `earnedBasisRegistry` and leaving a leg ungraded only where the record earned nothing. So volume does not multiply a decision nobody can make, and the composed-at-volume case is not a wider version of this debt. (b) **BUT THE FIRST DEFENCE DEGRADES WITH VOLUME WHILE THE ARITHMETIC DOES NOT.** *"Every ungraded leg is NAMED"* is a defence only while somebody reads the names; one ungraded leg among four is seen, forty among two hundred in a machine-composed reading is a list nobody reads. The rule holds; its enforcement by a human reader thins. (c) **AND A SIBLING LEVER NOW EXISTS THAT D-159 COULD NOT HAVE NAMED:** under DEC-32's OR-max a leg can be put in a BRANCH OF ITS OWN, where it neither floors nor unrates — visibly present and contributing nothing, which is a stronger form of the same move. That is DEC-32's own named hazard with its own three containments (authored sufficiency, the compound falsifier, the reader), so it is a sibling and not an extension. **WHAT THE RE-READING CHANGES IS THE OBSERVABLE, not the rule:** this row says to watch whether groups leave cuts-against legs ungraded; against a machine the fact worth measuring is the RATIO AND ITS PROVENANCE — how many of a reading's legs are ungraded, and whether each is ungraded because THE RECORD EARNED NOTHING or because A MEMBER DID NOT GRADE IT. Those are one fact today and will not be. **PL-14 makes that measurement cheap rather than taking it:** `op=versionstrength` publishes `ungraded[]` with a per-leg reason distinguishing the two, so when M10 runs with a real group the number this row waits for can be read off the answer instead of reconstructed. Answering it needs a model of each action kind's preconditions, which is procedural, jurisdiction-specific, and nowhere in the record. **DEFERRED 2026-08-03 by Bob** — *"may also [be] too difficult to take on now"* — **with the derivation path named, which is what makes this deferrable rather than open-ended:** *"it could be figured out through some combination of supporting evidence already gathered, mechanical means, and AI support."* Three bounded inputs: what the project has ALREADY established (the gap is relative to a real corpus, not an abstract standard); MECHANICAL checks for the structural preconditions (a filing window against a date, standing against who the group is, exhaustion against actions already recorded); and AI for the procedural knowledge that is neither. **What this does NOT block: the plan itself.** A plan can be authored, ordered, branched, costed and support-labelled with the member supplying what an action requires; only the system answering the backward question on its own waits. | D-129 | design | 2026-08-01 | **`undetermined` conflates two different claims: WE DO NOT KNOW, and THERE IS POSITIVELY NONE.** Found by the practice survey (`PRACTICE-SURVEY.md`): Wikidata distinguishes `somevalue` (unknown) from `novalue` (positively no value) as separate ASSERTED statements, and BIO blurs them under one word. The conflation is real in our own code, not theoretical — a scanned PDF with no text layer is `novalue` (there is no text to extract, permanently, a property of the document) while a PDF over the Worker envelope is `somevalue` (there IS text and we did not get it, a limitation of our run that a later run could lift). Both currently surface as text-undetermined with the difference only in a reason string. The same split is latent elsewhere: an authority nobody can establish versus a document that genuinely has no issuing authority; a link whose verdict cannot be established versus one established to have no relationship. WHY IT MATTERS BEYOND TIDINESS: the standing rule is that `undetermined` is first-class and must be STATED, and its value to a reader is that it says what we do not know — but 'we could not read this' and 'there is nothing here to read' are different claims about the world, and a member deciding whether to re-capture, escalate or move on needs to tell them apart. One is worth retrying; the other never will be. Cheapest correct shape is probably a second field beside the reason rather than a new vocabulary, but that is a design call for whoever takes it. — PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim at the close: M2 - open **WIDENED 2026-08-04 by the archival-systems survey (`STORE-AS-CACHE.md`): the useful state set is LARGER than two, and `partial` is the state BIO most obviously lacks.** Software Heritage stores crawl outcomes explicitly as `OriginVisitStatus.status` in {`created`, `not_found`, `ongoing`, `failed`, `partial`, `full`} — so *we went to this origin at time T and it was not there* is stored DATA, never an inference from missing rows (docs.softwareheritage.org/devel/apidoc/swh.storage.interface.html). Memento (RFC 7089) goes further and makes an archived 404 a CITABLE Memento, MANDATING that a replayed 4XX/5XX carry the same status code, with CDX indexing it in `statuscode` beside the 200s. **`partial` is not hypothetical for us: it is CPDF-5's measured Tier-1-at-88% case and every over-envelope document**, both of which today collapse into the same `undetermined` as a document we never read at all. The governing finding of that survey bears directly on this row — *systems built as ARCHIVES record absence; systems built as CACHES treat absence as a transient failure to be retried* — so this row is load-bearing for the read-through-cache framing rather than tidiness. **SHARPENED AGAIN 2026-08-04 by the caching-systems survey: the principled set is FOUR states with GRADED RETENTION, and there is an AUTHORITY rule BIO already believes in another vocabulary.** RFC 2308 (*Negative Caching of DNS Queries*) separates: the name does not exist (NXDOMAIN, SOA-derived retention, *"one to three hours… work well"*, over a day *"problematic"*); exists-but-not-this-record (NODATA); **we asked and could not tell (SERVFAIL — MUST NOT be cached longer than five minutes)**; and the server was unreachable (same five-minute bound). So: `NEVER_LOOKED` / `LOOKED_ABSENT` / `LOOKED_INDETERMINATE` / `PRESENT`, plus SWH's `partial`. **An indeterminate result must never inherit a definitive lifetime** — CloudFront's error floor is 10 s, Google Cloud CDN publishes 120 s for 404/410/451 and 60 s for 405/501. HTTP encodes the same confidence split more weakly and has NO wire representation for *we have not looked*, which is exactly this row's conflation. **AND RFC 2308 §5 IS THIS PROJECT'S OWN DOCTRINE, ARRIVED AT INDEPENDENTLY IN 1998: *"Negative responses without SOA records SHOULD NOT be cached"*** — the SOA is the proof of AUTHORITY that makes a negative answer a FINDING rather than an absence of finding, so an absence with no authority attached is not recordable at all. That is *an equality that costs nothing to produce is not evidence*, applied to absence. | M2 · CLOSED 2026-09-23 AS A DEBT ROW by LED-7 (SCHEDULER #14) on BOB #29's ruling — door 1 NARROWED with door 3 for the residue. BUILT IN FACT: the state set is `BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md` §14.3's table, carried by `observation_log.state` (LOOKED_ABSENT, LOOKED_INDETERMINATE, partial, PRESENT; NEVER_LOOKED the absence of a row, `airun.mjs` OBSERVATION_STATES), and REC-91's content writer separates the row's own example (over the bound reads partial, no unit carrying text reads LOOKED_ABSENT, no unit arm reads LOOKED_INDETERMINATE). The residue, GRADED RETENTION, is deferred in §14.3 with its trigger (the first unattended consumer that re-looks a subject from its latest observation). Verified on main `7abfbe54`. |

| D-219 | defect | 2026-08-06 | **A stored, member-facing method string says "with no captured basis" and reads as "with no basis", which is FALSE — the basis is REQUIRED.** Found 2026-08-06 while answering Bob's question *"What is testimony with no basis? Isn't there always context for how something got into the system?"* — and his instinct was right. `testifyResolution` (`store.mjs:8398`) REFUSES without one: `NO_BASIS` — *"grade D is recorded testimony: it carries the member's stated basis, with an author and a date"*. So a grade-D testimony always carries the member's stated reason, an author and a date. What it lacks is a CAPTURED DOCUMENT supporting the link, which is why it grades weakest. **But the method string written into the record reads** `testimony -- asserted by <who> with no captured basis (framework 8.1 grade D)` **, and a reader meeting "no captured basis" hears "no basis at all" — the record describing its own contents more weakly than they are.** This is the inverse of the usual defect class here (a comment claiming a stronger property than the code enforces); it is a stored string claiming a WEAKER one, and it is worse than a comment because it is member-facing and persisted on every grade-D resolution. NEEDS: the string to say what is actually missing — no captured document — while keeping the grade and the framework reference. Also worth a pass for siblings: any other place where "no basis" is used to mean "no captured basis". **Prior disposition, verbatim:** M8 · open — one string plus its assertions; it rides whatever next touches the resolution path · SENT TO BOB 2026-09-23 by LED-7 batch S15-1 (SCHEDULER #15) (DELEGATION of the same date): the stored string quotes the design's own grade-D label, `BIO_Content_Framework_v0_10.md` §8.1's table cell *asserted with no captured basis*, and `schema.mjs` repeats it twice, so the rewording is the design's first. | D-276 | defect | 2026-08-09 | **`agent-worker` ASKS `op=meaningrows` FOR AN ARM THAT DOES NOT EXIST, AND TURNS THE PLANES REFUSAL INTO A CONFIDENT ZERO.** Found by D-258 while measuring who actually consumes that op, and **DRIVEN through the real op rather than read**: `agent-worker/src/index.mjs` passes `rows: "legs"` at all three call sites (the `meaningRead` default at :453, `collect` at :543, `compose` at :562), and the compilers arms are `leg`, `resolves`, `concerns`. Against a live plane `op=meaningrows&rows=legs` answers **`ok:false, reason: MEANING_ROWS_UNKNOWN_ARM, check: C-23.2`** — a refusal whose own translation says it exists so an answer from a different kind cannot *"look complete"*. **The caller never checks `ok`.** It tests `.reached` (transport only), then computes `Array.isArray(got.rows) ? got.rows.length : 0` and writes the run note **`0 meaning-grain row(s) queried; no document was loaded`**. So the one fence built to stop a false sense of coverage is converted, one line later, into exactly the false sense of coverage it was built to stop — and it lands in an AI runs OBSERVATION ENTRIES, which are record. This is CLAUDE.mds *"absence at one level is not evidence of absence at the next"* and *"a defect that makes the record claim more than it can support"*, in the same call. **ITS OWN SUITE CANNOT SEE IT, WHICH IS THE SECOND HALF OF THE FINDING:** `agent-worker/test/agent-worker.test.mjs:164` mocks the op as `{ ok: true, rows: [] }` **for any `rows` argument whatsoever**, so the battery is green over a call that cannot succeed against a real plane — the hand-copy-agrees-for-free class this project has now measured six times. **NOT FIXED HERE: `agent-worker/**` is FLEETs** (FL-5s claim) and D-258 is RECORDs; see the DELEGATION in `CLAIMS.md`. **Closing it is small and has three parts, and the third is the one that matters:** spell the arm `leg`; check `ok` and treat a refusal as a REFUSAL rather than an empty answer (the note must be able to say the record was not asked, which is a different sentence from nothing was found); and make the mock refuse an unknown arm the way the plane does, or the fix is invisible to the suite that is supposed to hold it. **Prior disposition, verbatim:** M3 · open — **measured and DRIVEN 2026-08-09** (`MEANING_ROWS_UNKNOWN_ARM` observed live, `rows.length` computed as 0); delegated to FLEET | M3 · CLOSED 2026-09-23 IN FACT by LED-7 batch S15-2 (SCHEDULER #15): fixed at `954d5fef` (an ancestor of `origin/main`), verified on `b5ce975a`: `agent-worker/src/harness.mjs` exports `MEANING_ARM = "leg"`, the arm the compiler holds, and `agent-worker/src/index.mjs` treats a call as refused when either the envelope or the op's own object says no, so a `MEANING_ROWS_UNKNOWN_ARM` answer is no longer written as a zero. |

| D-190 | gap | 2026-08-04 | **A Durable Object's SQLite is capped at 10 GB per object and this ceiling is recorded nowhere in the repository.** VENDOR CLAIM (developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/platform/limits, retrieved 2026-08-04): 10 GB per object on Workers Paid, 1 GB on Free; unlimited objects; a soft ~1,000 req/s per object. D-36 records workerd's SQL STATEMENT limits (100 bound params, 5 compound-SELECT terms) and M6 names R2 growth and the free-tier storage ceiling, but the DO's own storage ceiling — the ceiling on THE RECORD, as distinct from the captured bytes in R2 — appears in no row and no measurement. Today that is abstract because every instance is a development instance. **Under the read-through-cache framing (`STORE-AS-CACHE.md`) it stops being abstract, because the store then grows BY DESIGN rather than by member action.** At some corpus size the record must sharded across objects, and the single-threaded ~1,000 req/s per object is a second ceiling on the same decision. Deciding it now is cheap; deciding it after a cache model ships is the D-113/scheduler class — a shape question settled late, with consumers already built on the wrong assumption. NOT MEASURED BY US: the vendor figure is a claim, and what matters is the growth CURVE per captured document, which nothing has measured. **Prior disposition, verbatim:** M6 · **PARTLY MEASURED 2026-08-08 (FL-1, riding D-218s measurement — same `MEASUREMENTS.md` entry).** Instrument: the planes own `op=stats` -> `dbBytes`, i.e. workerds `ctx.storage.sql.databaseSize` (`src/store.mjs:11209`), read live on two namespaces. **Empty-schema baseline 925,696 B; loaded store 6,402,048 B at 31 bundles / 142 files / 88 register / 244 history -> 176,657 B per bundle**, which against the vendors 10 GB claim is ~60,800 bundles and against 1 GB ~6,073 (numerator ours, denominator theirs). **ALSO MEASURED, and it is why this row stays OPEN: the platform has NO per-object storage surface at all** — `durableObjectsStorageGroups` carries only date dimensions and returned zero rows on this account, and `durableObjectsPeriodicGroups` is per-object but reports no stored bytes, so `dbBytes` off the object is the only handle there is. **AND THE MEASURED POINT HAS AN EMPTY MEANING LAYER — connections, entities and resolutions are all ZERO — so the slope prices CAPTURE ONLY and is a FLOOR on cost / an OVERSTATEMENT of capacity.** What remains is exactly D-224s measurement: the quadratic `connections` growth on the same object. Do not read 60,800 as headroom until that lands | M6 · CLOSED 2026-09-23 by LED-7 batch S15-4 (SCHEDULER #15), door 3 — A STATED LIMITATION IN ITS HOME DESIGN: `docs/development/STORE-AS-CACHE.md` states the 10 GB per-object ceiling (Paid; 1 GB Free) as a vendor claim under "What the platform permits", and its measurement list, item 2, names the DO storage curve per captured document against that ceiling as the number that decides when the record must shard. FL-1's partial measurement stands (176,657 B per bundle at 31 bundles, about 60,800 bundles against the vendor's 10 GB). The sharding decision is owed when the store grows by design under the cache model, and item 2 is its trigger. That section's sentence calling the ceiling recorded nowhere is now stale, a note for BOB at the next corpus pass. |

So each of the two sessions has now silently taken the other's work, neither noticed at the time, and it was recorded in a COMMIT MESSAGE where no later session would look for it — which is the same "a note is not an item" class as D-292. With it, a layer whose producer names OCR software becomes `layer -> ocr(<product>)` with the product NAMED and the chain honest, and a layer with NO such marker stays `undetermined` — **never "authored", because an absent marker is an absent marker.** That last clause is the whole design and is why this is not a lookup table of OCR product names: the question cannot be answered in principle from a name string, so the DEFAULT must be undetermined and the classification must only ever be able to make the claim WEAKER. NEEDS: the `/Info` read in `pdfstructure.mjs` (CONTENT-PDF's own path, ~30 lines), a `producer` field on I2's text shape (additive, an IC), and the chain step composed from it in `index.mjs`'s acquire assembly. Relation: CPDF-9's third amendment to DEC-4, CPDF-10, D-152, CPDF-13 (a producer-named engine is a calibration SUBJECT). **PRIOR DISPOSITION, moved verbatim by LED-7 batch S16-3:** M2 · **BUILT 2026-08-10 by CONTENT-PDF (session `cpdf-d251`), on the area's own branch, green — CONDUCT integrates and CONDUCT closes the row.** The `/Info` read is in `pdfstructure.mjs` (both trailer shapes: the classic `trailer` dict AND the xref STREAM the Legistar class actually uses, plus an `/Info` compressed into an `/ObjStm`); `text.producer` is on I2's text shape (IC-58, ADDITIVE, filed with measured consumer impact); the chain step is composed in `index.mjs`'s acquire assembly, appended and never substituted. **`determination` has exactly two values, frozen, and "authored" is not one of them** — the negative control's second arm makes the classification able to strengthen and SIX assertions fail naming it. **VERIFIED ON THE LIVE RECORD the same day:** the three documents this row was raised about — Oakland's enacted certified resolutions 89484, 89498 and 89518 CMS — were re-fetched from Legistar and all three now read `layer -> ocr(ABBYY FineReader Engine 11)`, while the 8 other attachments on those same matters read `undetermined` (zero false positives). MEASUREMENTS.md 2026-08-10. **Residual, named rather than left to be found:** XMP `CreatorTool` is still not read (CPDF-9 measured it never disagreeing with `/Info`), and an OCR tool that overwrites `/Info` with its own PDF-writer name stays `undetermined` — which is the honest answer, not a missed detection | M2 · CLOSED 2026-09-23 IN FACT by LED-7 batch S16-3 (SCHEDULER #16), door 1 — the cheap read this row asked for is on `main`, read at the code on `0e7cc03e`: `bio-plane/src/pdfstructure.mjs` reads the trailer's `/Info` (its D-251 section, both trailer shapes and an `/Info` inside an `/ObjStm`), the text shape carries `producer`, and `index.mjs`'s acquire assembly appends the step only when the producer's `determination` is `ocr` and names a product, never substituting. The prior disposition's two residuals are measured scope, not defects: XMP `CreatorTool` is not read because CPDF-9's 19-document sweep found it never disagreeing with `/Info` (`MEASUREMENTS.md`, 2026-08-10; the reason stated in `pdfstructure.mjs`'s header), and an OCR tool that overwrites `/Info` reads `undetermined`, which is the record stating what it cannot tell. |

| D-121 | gap | 2026-07-31 | **Spreadsheets, word-processing and presentation documents are captured as bytes only, and the format dispatch cannot absorb them.** Bob's direction: BIO must inspect these as it inspects HTML and PDF, cleanly. Research in `OFFICE-FORMATS.md`. The shape of the finding: `.docx`/`.xlsx`/`.pptx` are ONE container (OOXML = ZIP of XML parts) with three part-maps, and `.odt`/`.ods`/`.odp` are the same shape — so this is not three parsers. MEASURED 2026-07-31 in workerd via miniflare: `DecompressionStream` supports `deflate-raw` and round-trips it (480 raw / 29 compressed / intact), so the container is readable in the plane with ZERO dependency, exactly as `FlateDecode` made PDF phase 1 dependency-free. Outbound links live uniformly in `_rels/*.rels` with `TargetMode="External"` and map onto I2's existing partitions unchanged; embedded files are `intra`. Text is EASIER than PDF, not harder — XML text nodes, no glyph/CMap problem, so there is no Tier 2 and no fleet member for office text. Two element references are BETTER than PDF's page+rect: `Sheet1!B14` and slide+shape are stable and human-meaningful, the first time the record can cite finer than a document without inventing an anchor scheme. THE ARCHITECTURAL POINT: today's dispatch is already TWO mechanisms that do not scale — a hardcoded `HTML_CT` array guarding the acquire-time subresource branch (everything else refused `NOT_HTML`) and a separate read-time `op=pdfstructure`. Three more formats makes five special cases across two mechanisms. The framework already specifies the fix and has never exercised it: §4's uniform recogniser plus a registry per axis, with FORMAT named outright as a candidate axis. So the work is stand up the FORMAT registry and MOVE HTML AND PDF ONTO IT, then add formats as registry entries.
